# Game-day write-up: 2026-11-02 (US Eastern)

Injury report 2026-11-02 17:15:00 ET · odds snapshot 20261102T222146Z (Sportsbet, TAB, Dabble, Betr, Neds, Ladbrokes, PointsBet; US books for reference).

**How to read this**: *Model fair* = the projection alone: minutes as a scenario mix, per-minute rates lined up with tonight's lineup (absences in and out), opponent pace and defence (points, assists), opponent rebounding, and potentials / rebound chances. EV = best AU price × model probability − 1; **value** = EV ≥ 10% on a rung priced $3–$10. No backtest adjustments. SGM fair prices are model-only: compare with the builder.
